- **telegram**: 📌 **TL;DR:** - **Problem:** your AI is working on your computer, gets stuck on a question ("deploy now?"), and waits until you are back at your desk. - **Outcome:** it sends you the question on Telegram; you tap **Reply** and your answer goes back to the same AI conversation. - **Start:** ask it what it can do: `agent-bus help '#telegram/[email removed]'` ([Try this first](#try-this-first)). ([parf/ai-agent-bus](https://github.com/parf/ai-agent-bus/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/telegram.md) · ★1)
- **telegram**: The owner's phone, wired to an agent that has the repository. One workflow (`.github/workflows/telegram.yml`), one entry script (`scripts/telegram-bridge.mjs`) over `scripts/telegram/*`, one Edge Function (`supabase/functions/telegram`). Design and reasoning: [ADR 0129](../adr/0129-the-telegram-bot-is-an-agent.md) (why it is an agent, and what replaced the commands), [ADR 0122](../adr/0122-the-web ([royaraqamia/royaraqamia](https://github.com/royaraqamia/royaraqamia/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/telegram.md))

Имя и username не участвуют в идентификации. Аккаунт с почтой и Telegram-аккаунт одного человека остаются отдельными, автоматического объединения нет. Email-вход сохраняет прежнее поведение. - **tenant-isolation-reviewer**: Reviews a diff for breaches of tenant isolation in OPOS - RLS policies and grants in migrations, tenant columns and composite foreign keys in the schema, cross-tenant queries, the tenant transaction and permission checks in the API. Use before opening a PR that touches packages/db (schema, migrations, scripts) or apps/api, and on request for a branch or a PR number. Read-only; reports findings, never edits. ([opos-land/opos](https://github.com/opos-land/opos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tenant-isolation-reviewer.md))
- **tenant-isolation-reviewer**: Convexの公開関数(query/mutation/action)にテナント分離・認可の漏れがないかを検査する読み取り専用レビュアー。convex/配下を変更したPRのレビュー時や各フェーズ完了時に使う。 ([yamk12nfu/warikapp](https://github.com/yamk12nfu/warikapp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tenant-isolation-reviewer.md))
- **tenant-isolation-reviewer**: Revisa rotas de API, queries Prisma e cache do cliente em busca de vazamento de dados entre clínicas, usuários ou pacientes. Use após mexer em src/app/api, src/lib ou no cache. ([Yuurij-dev/Clinica-Harmonize-plus](https://github.com/Yuurij-dev/Clinica-Harmonize-plus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tenant-isolation-reviewer.md))
- **tenant-isolation-tests**: Multi-tenant sem testes explícitos de isolamento é uma bomba relógio. Este agente gera testes automatizados que PROVAM: tenant A não vê dados de tenant B, OPERACIONAL não vê dados de outros OPERACIONAIS, cache não vaza entre tenants, ID enumeration retorna 404 (não 403), RLS Postgres está ativo em todas as tabelas. Bloqueia release se qualquer teste falhar. ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/tenant-isolation-tests.md))
- **tenant-layers**: A Tenant is a Nuxt layer under `layers/&lt;tenant&gt;/`; Nuxt auto-extends every `layers/*`, so there is no `extends` list (ADR-0018). The short layer instructions (auto-imports, aliases, adding a Tenant, what leaks) live in `.claude/rules/tenant-layers.md`; this doc holds the longer ones. ([feffef/terrarium](https://github.com/feffef/terrarium/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/tenant-layers.md) · ★1)
- **tenant-leak-auditor**: Audite le code à la recherche de fuites de données entre établissements (multi-tenant) — requêtes non filtrées par tenant, tenantId venant d'une entrée utilisateur, clés de cache ou chemins S3 sans tenant, endpoints renvoyant 403 au lieu de 404. À lancer avant de merger un module métier, ou quand on soupçonne un problème d'isolation. ([TMap-Works/spa-booking](https://github.com/TMap-Works/spa-booking/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tenant-leak-auditor.md))
- **tenant-leak-critic**: Use proactively before merging a PR that touches tenant-bearing code, or when investigating a cross-tenant leak. Automatically delegate to this agent when: - A Terraform file was just written or modified and needs quality review - User asks to "review", "check", "audit", or "lint" a Terraform module or resource - New IaC resources are introduced (especially IAM, network, storage, secrets) - Terraform code touching production infrastructure or security-sensitive paths is changed Returns a structured review report; the orchestrator decides whether to apply fixes.
